Generated by `build_document_index.py`. Do not edit this file manually.
It indexes maintained documents under `docs/` with strictly more than 5000 words (whitespace-separated tokens). Exactly 5000 words does not qualify. This file is a locator, not a substitute for reading the applicable rules.

```text
python build_document_index.py

python build_document_index.py list
```
